## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] Hi, I'm Ducter, your partner for work on this machine. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 맡아서 처리해 드려요. 예를 들어, 특정 프로젝트에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 작업을 열어 진행하고 결과를 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 오늘 얘기한 것 중 남은 건 그때 이어서 챙길게요.
  [also used tools] routine_list
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 알겠어요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くことを保証する」、UDPは「速さを優先して、保証はしない」という違いです。

TCPは相手と接続を確立し、届いたかを確認しながら順番どおりに送ります。失敗すれば再送もします。その分、遅延は増えます。UDPは接続も確認もせず、データをそのまま送ります。途中で消えても再送されません。その代わり軽くて速く、リアルタイム性が求められる通話、動画配信、オンラインゲームなどでよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, por ejemplo para limpiar una rama propia o ponerla al día con la rama principal. Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual su historia, como al integrar un trabajo terminado en la rama principal.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的：先握手，再保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 不做这些，只管把包尽快发出去，快但不保证送达。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not pushed yet, so you can tidy commits or pull in the latest main for a clean, linear history.

Merge when others already work on the branch or it's been shared, because rebasing rewrites commits and breaks their copies. Merging keeps the history as it really happened.
```

A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

路径不用写全，`bun test foo` 会跑所有文件名里带 `foo` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "某个测试名"
```

两者可以组合，比如 `bun test foo.test.ts -t "某个测试名"`。
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 compaction 那个 PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细，改好了告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

已定的事：沿用这个 PR 原来的分支和工作目录，接着做，不新开 PR。
完成标准：1）按 review 里关于锁粒度的意见缩小加锁范围，并说明改了哪几处；2）相关测试和已有测试都跑过，把结果贴进报告；3）改动提交在这个任务的分支上。
不做的事：不合并，不推送到主分支，不改草稿 PR 的状态，不处理 review 里与锁无关的意见。
如果 review 里的锁粒度意见有两种理解，选更保守的那种，并在报告里写明。
```

}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();

</div>

  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>

  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 打车"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>

</details>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? fmt(total)+"，"+xs.length+" 笔":"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what,yuan,day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-baby-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 做个小应用记宝宝喝奶。今天早上 6:10 喝了 120ml，9:05 喝了 90ml
  [partner message] 做好了，今天的两次已经填进去了，合计 210 ml。下次喝奶记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 5441 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝了</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
</section>

<h2 class="k-h">记录</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记。记下第一次喝奶，它会出现在这里。</div>
</div>
<p class="k-from" id="hint">点一条记录可以修改或删除。</p>

<details class="k-add" id="add">
  <summary id="addTitle">记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-field"><label for="time">时间</label><input class="k-input" id="time" placeholder="比如 14:30" maxlength="5"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="比如 120"></div>
    <div class="k-chips" id="chips">
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-ml="60">60</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-ml="90">90</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-ml="120">120</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-ml="150">150</button>
    </div>
    <div class="k-field" style="display:flex;gap:8px">
      <button class="k-btn primary" id="save" type="button" style="flex:1">记下</butt … setChips(){
    var v = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    document.querySelectorAll('#chips .k-chip').forEach(function(c){
      c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(v === +c.getAttribute('data-ml')));
    });
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('#chips .k-chip').forEach(function(c){
    c.onclick = function(){
      $('ml').value = c.getAttribute('data-ml');
      setChips();
    };
  });
  $('ml').oninput = setChips;

  $('save').onclick = function(){
    var t = parseTime($('time').value);
    var ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!t) { $('time').focus(); return; }
    if (!(ml > 0)) { $('ml').focus(); return; }
    if (editId) {
      feeds = feeds.map(function(f){
        return f.id === editId ? { id: f.id, d: f.d, t: t, ml: ml } : f;
      });
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6), d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
    }
    persist();
    clearForm();
    $('add').open = false;
    render();
  };

  $('del').onclick = function(){
    feeds = feeds.filter(function(f){ return f.id !== editId; });
    persist();
    clearForm();
    $('add').open = false;
    render();
  };

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"a1","d":"2026-11-21","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"a2","d":"2026-11-21","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four waiting PRs. Tap one when it lands and it gets crossed off, and the count updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1972 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs · tap one when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row.is-done .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row.is-done .k-row-value { color: var(--d-good); }
  .k-tick { width: 22px; height: 22px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1.5px solid var(--d-line); display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-right: 10px; font-size: 14px; color: var(--d-good); flex: none; }
  .k-row.is-done .k-tick { border-color: var(--d-good); }
  .k-row { cursor: pointer; }
</style>
<script>
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var done = ducter.get("done") || {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    PRS.forEach(function (p) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row" + (done[p.id] ? " is-done" : "");
      row.innerHTML =
        '<span class="k-tick">' + (done[p.id] ? "✓" : "") + '</span>' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + p.title + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + p.repo + ' · ' + p.waiting + '</div>';
      row.addEventListener("click", function () {
        done[p.id] = !done[p.id];
        ducter.set("done", done);
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var left = PRS.filter(function (p) { return !done[p.id]; }).length;
    var landed = PRS.length - left;
    document.getElementById("left").textContent = left;
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = "of " + PRS.length + " PRs · " + landed + " landed";
    ducter.summary(left + " waiting · " + landed + " landed");
  }

  ducter.onChange(function () {
    done = ducter.get("done") || {};
    render();
  });
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","waiting":"Weizhen's review, since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","waiting":"CI, currently failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","waiting":"Alice, since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","waiting":"Draft, not yet asked for review"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

      <div class="k-chips" id="cats">
        <button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button>
        <button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button>
        <button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="餐饮">餐饮</button>
        <button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="其他">其他</button>
      </div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
      <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
      <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这笔</button> … ck = () => edit(i);
    const m = document.createElement('span');
    m.className = 'k-row-main';
    m.textContent = x.trip || '未命名';
    const d = document.createElement('span');
    d.className = 'k-row-sub';
    d.textContent = x.day || '';
    const c = document.createElement('span');
    c.className = 'k-row-sub';
    c.textContent = x.cat || '';
    const v = document.createElement('span');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = fmt(x.amt);
    r.append(m, d, c, v);
    list.append(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total) : '还没记');
}

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!amt) return;
  const xs = ducter.get('items') || [];
  const it = { trip: $('trip').value.trim() || '北京出差', day: $('day').value.trim(), cat: cat, amt: amt };
  if (editing >= 0) xs[editing] = it; else xs.push(it);
  ducter.set('items', xs);
  $('form').open = false;
  reset();
  draw();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  const xs = ducter.get('items') || [];
  if (editing < 0) return;
  xs.splice(editing, 1);
  ducter.set('items', xs);
  $('form').open = false;
  reset();
  draw();
};

ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}

```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台米色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑，屏幕空白，边框是深藏青色，键盘和触控板画得很细。背景是透明的。

适合放在空状态里。空白的屏幕正好表示"还没有内容"，米色和柔和的画风也显得亲切，不会让人觉得冷清或出错了。

用的时候有两点要注意：
- 原图只有 320×246，空状态里一般显示在 160 到 200 像素左右，清楚度够用。如果要在高分屏上显示，最好换成两倍大的版本。
- 深色模式下，深藏青色的边框可能和深色背景混在一起，需要实际看一下。米色的机身一般没问题。
```
